{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Kndi9otHurzXhogjvKLfTJ","type":"tool_result","content":"# Coral NPU\n\nCoral NPU is a hardware accelerator for ML inferencing. Coral NPU is an Open Source IP designed by Google Research and is freely available for integration into ultra-low-power System-on-Chips (SoCs) targeting wearable devices such as hearables, augmented reality (AR) glasses and smart watches.\n\nCoral NPU is a neural processing unit (NPU), also known as an AI accelerator or deep-learning processor. Coral NPU is based on the 32-bit RISC-V Instruction Set Architecture (ISA).\n\nCoral NPU includes three distinct processor components that work together: matrix, vector (SIMD), and scalar.\n\n![Coral Initial state is the full sequence\n// 0, 1, ..., capacity-1, all available.\nclass IndexAllocatorShifting(c: Int) extends IndexAllocator(c) {\n  // entries(0) is the head (next to allocate)\n  val entries = RegInit(VecInit((0 until capacity).map(_.U(idxW.W))))\n  val nFree   = RegInit(capacity.U(log2Ceil(capacity + 1).W))\n\n  // Allocation is available when there is at least one free entry.\n  io.alloc.valid := nFree =/= 0.U\n  io.alloc.bits  := entries(0)\n\n  // Free is ready when there is space (i.e. not all entries free).\n  io.free.ready  := nFree =/= capacity.U\n\n  val doAlloc = io.alloc.valid && io.alloc.ready\n  val doFree  = io.free.valid  && io.free.ready\n\n  // Decide how many shifts (0 or 1) and whether to write at the end.\n  val shifted = Wire(Vec(capacity, UInt(idxW.W)))\n  for (i <- 0 until capacity) {\n    shifted(i) := Mux(doAlloc,\n                      if (i == capacity - 1) entries(i) else entries(i + 1),\n                      entries(i))\n  }\n\n  // Position to write a freed index into.\n  // If we just shifted (allocated), the new tail position is nFree - 1.\n  // Otherwise it's nFree.\n  val writePos = Mux(doAlloc, nFree - 1.U, nFree)\n\n  when (doFree) {\n    shifted(writePos) := io.free.bits\n  }\n\n  entries := shifted\n\n  val nNext = Wire(UInt(log2Ceil(capacity + 1).W))\n  nNext := nFree\n  when ( doAlloc && !doFree) { nNext := nFree - 1.U }\n  when (!doAlloc &&  doFree) { nNext := nFree + 1.U }\n  nFree := nNext\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1301,"cache_read_input_tokens":101310,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1301},"output_tokens":58,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c7ddc2fe-6b01-40a3-8e46-daa08a1b99f9","uuid":"ed96dd8e-b4e3-4c31-9b6b-8ba67b2d2b38"}

Pops from head on\n// allocate; pushes to tail on free. Initial state is the full sequence\n// 0, 1, ..., capacity-1, all available.\nclass IndexAllocatorShifting(c: Int) extends IndexAllocator(c) {\n  // entries(0) is the head (next to allocate)\n  val entries = RegInit(VecInit((0 until capacity).map(_.U(idxW.W))))\n  val nFree   = RegInit(capacity.U(log2Ceil(capacity + 1).W))\n\n  // Allocation is available when there is at least one free entry.\n  io.alloc.valid := nFree =/= 0.U\n  io.alloc.bits  := entries(0)\n\n  // Free is ready when there is space (i.e. not all entries free).\n  io.free.ready  := nFree =/= capacity.U\n\n  val doAlloc = io.alloc.valid && io.alloc.ready\n  val doFree  = io.free.valid  && io.free.ready\n\n  // Decide how many shifts (0 or 1) and whether to write at the end.\n  val shifted = Wire(Vec(capacity, UInt(idxW.W)))\n  for (i <- 0 until capacity) {\n    shifted(i) := Mux(doAlloc,\n                      if (i == capacity - 1) entries(i) else entries(i + 1),\n                      entries(i))\n  }\n\n  // Position to write a freed index into.\n  // If we just shifted (allocated), the new tail position is nFree - 1.\n  // Otherwise it's nFree.\n  val writePos = Mux(doAlloc, nFree - 1.U, nFree)\n\n  when (doFree) {\n    shifted(writePos) := io.free.bits\n  }\n\n  entries := shifted\n\n  val nNext = Wire(UInt(log2Ceil(capacity + 1).W))\n  nNext := nFree\n  when ( doAlloc && !doFree) { nNext := nFree - 1.U }\n  when (!doAlloc &&  doFree) { nNext := nFree + 1.U }\n  nFree := nNext\n}\n","userModified":false}}

1, 0)\n    } else {\n      Mux(idx >= capacity.U, idx - capacity.U, idx)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def enqueue(data: Vec[T], k: UInt): FIFOState[T] = {\n    val next = Wire(new FIFOState(gen, capacity))\n    next.head := this.head\n    next.tail := wrap(this.tail +& k)\n    next.count := (this.count +& k)(cntW - Copyright 2025 Google LLC\n//\n// Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n// you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n// You may obtain a copy of the License at\n//\n//     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n\npackage common\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nclass CoralNPURRArbiter[T <: Data](gen: T, n: Int) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val in     = Vec(n, Flipped(Decoupled(gen)))\n    val out    = Decoupled(gen)\n    val chosen = Output(UInt(log2Ceil(n).W))\n  })\n\n  // Round-robin: prefer indices > lastGrant, falling back to <= lastGrant.\n  val lastGrant = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(n).W))\n\n  // For each index i, a priority based on round-robin starting after lastGrant.\n  // We compute the {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HMZHCcwJUDdMaE7AsHUnAm","type":"tool_result","content":"#include <gtest/gtest.h>\n\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"spi_master.h\"\n\nclass MockFtdi : public FtdiInterface {\n public:\n  std::vector<uint8_t> written;\n  std::vector<uint8_t> to_read;\n  size_t read_idx = 0;\n\n  int write_data(const uint8_t* buf, int size) override {\n    written.insert(written.end(), buf, buf + size);\n    return size;\n  }\n\n  int read_data(uint8_t* buf, int size) override {\n    int n = 0;\n    while (n < size && read_idx < to_read.size()) {\n      buf[n++] = to_read[read_idx++];\n    }\n    return n;\n  }\n\n  int purge_buffers() override {\n    // Keep written commands for verification in tests that span multiple calls.\n    return 0;\n  }\n};\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, WriteLines) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n  uint8_t data[16] = {0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44, 0x55, 0x66, 0x77, 0x88,\n                      0x99, 0xAA, 0xBB, 0xCC, 0xDD, 0xEE, 0xFF, 0x00};\n\n  // Simulate read returning dummy byte for the drain wait loop\n  mock.to_read.push_back(0xFF);\n\n  spi.v2_write_lines(0x1000, data, 1);\n\n  // Assert header presence\n  ASSERT_GT(mock.written.size(), 20u);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[0], 0x80);  // CS Low\n}\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, ReadLines) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n\n  size_t window =\n      1 * SpiMaster::kBytesPerBeat + SpiMaster::kInitialLatencyPaddingBytes;\n  mock.to_read.resize(window, 0);\n  mock.to_read[5] = 0xFE;  // Sync token\n\n  for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {\n    mock.to_read[6 + i] = i + 1;  // 1, 2, 3... 16\n  }\n\n  uint8_t out[16] = {0};\n  bool res = spi.v2_read_lines(0x2000, 1, out);\n\n  EXPECT_TRUE(res);\n  EXPECT_EQ(out[0], 1);\n  EXPECT_EQ(out[15], 16);\n}\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, ReadLinesMultiBeat) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n\n  // Total window size in new implementation is num_beats * kBytesPerBeat +\n  // kInitialLatencyPaddingBytes\n  size_t window =\n      2 * SpiMaster::kBytesPerBeat + SpiMaster::kInitialLatencyPaddingBytes;\n  mock.to_read.resize(window, 0);\n\n  // Beat 0: sync at 100\n  mock.to_read[100] = 0xFE;\n  for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) mock.to_read[101 + i] = 0xA0 + i;\n\n  // Beat 1: sync at 200 (must be after beat 0's data)\n  mock.to_read[200] = 0xFE;\n  for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) mock.to_read[201 + i] = 0xB0 + i;\n\n  uint8_t out[32] = {0};\n  bool res = spi.v2_read_lines(0x3000, 2, out);\n\n  EXPECT_TRUE(res);\n  EXPECT_EQ(out[0], 0xA0);\n  EXPECT_EQ(out[15], 0xAF);\n  EXPECT_EQ(out[16], 0xB0);\n  EXPECT_EQ(out[31], 0xBF);\n}\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, WriteWord) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n\n  // write_word does RMW. First it reads 16 bytes.\n  // window = 1 * 17 + 2048 = 2065\n  size_t window =\n      1 * SpiMaster::kBytesPerBeat + SpiMaster::kInitialLatencyPaddingBytes;\n  mock.to_read.resize(window, 0xCC);\n  mock.to_read[500] = 0xFE;  // sync token\n\n  // For the v2_write_lines drain loop\n  mock.to_read.push_back(0xFF);\n\n  spi.write_word(0x4000, 0x12345678);\n\n  // mock.written will have:\n  // 1. Read command (v2_read_lines)\n  // 2. Write command (v2_write_lines)\n  // Search for the write command (Op=0x02)\n  size_t write_cmd_start = 0;\n  bool found = false;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < mock.written.size() - 7; ++i) {\n    if (mock.written[i] == 0x80 && mock.written[i + 6] == 0x02) {\n      write_cmd_start = i;\n      found = true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  ASSERT_TRUE(found);\n  // Payload is at write_cmd_start + 16\n  ASSERT_GT(mock.written.size(), write_cmd_start + 16u + 4u);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 16], 0x78);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 17], 0x56);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 18], 0x34);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 19], 0x12);\n  // Rest of the 16-byte line should still be 0xCC\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 20], 0xCC);\n}\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, WriteDataUnaligned) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n\n  // Address 0x1004, length 8. Bounds: 0x1000 to 0x1010 (1 beat)\n  // RMW will read 0x1000\n  size_t window =\n      1 * SpiMaster::kBytesPerBeat + SpiMaster::kInitialLatencyPaddingBytes;\n  mock.to_read.resize(window, 0xCC);\n  mock.to_read[500] = 0xFE;      // sync token\n  mock.to_read.push_back(0xFF);  // drain loop\n\n  uint8_t payload[8] = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8};\n  spi.v2_write_data(0x1004, payload, 8);\n\n  // Find write command\n  size_t write_cmd_start = 0;\n  bool found = false;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < mock.written.size() - 7; ++i) {\n    if (mock.written[i] == 0x80 && mock.written[i + 6] == 0x02) {\n      write_cmd_start = i;\n      found = true;\n    }\n  }\n  ASSERT_TRUE(found);\n  // Beat data starts at write_cmd_start + 16\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 16 + 0], 0xCC);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 16 + 3], 0xCC);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 16 + 4], 1);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 16 + 11], 8);\n  EXPECT_EQ(mock.written[write_cmd_start + 16 + 12], 0xCC);\n}\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, ReadDataUnaligned) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n\n  // Address 0x1004, length 8. Bounds: 0x1000 to 0x1010 (1 beat)\n  size_t window =\n      1 * SpiMaster::kBytesPerBeat + SpiMaster::kInitialLatencyPaddingBytes;\n  mock.to_read.resize(window, 0xCC);\n  mock.to_read[500] = 0xFE;  // sync token\n  for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {\n    mock.to_read[501 + i] = i;  // Data in line is 0, 1, 2, ... 15\n  }\n\n  uint8_t out[8] = {0};\n  bool res = spi.v2_read_data(0x1004, 8, out);\n\n  EXPECT_TRUE(res);\n  EXPECT_EQ(out[0], 4);\n  EXPECT_EQ(out[7], 11);\n}\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, ReadLinesTimeout) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n\n  // Return fewer bytes than window size (window = 1*17 + 2048 = 2065)\n  mock.to_read.resize(100, 0);\n\n  uint8_t out[16] = {0};\n  bool res = spi.v2_read_lines(0x2000, 1, out);\n\n  EXPECT_FALSE(res);\n}\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, ReadLinesMissingSync) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n\n  // Return full window size but no 0xFE sync token\n  size_t window =\n      1 * SpiMaster::kBytesPerBeat + SpiMaster::kInitialLatencyPaddingBytes;\n  mock.to_read.resize(window, 0xCC);\n\n  uint8_t out[16] = {0};\n  bool res = spi.v2_read_lines(0x2000, 1, out);\n\n  EXPECT_FALSE(res);\n}\n\nTEST(NexusLoaderTest, LargeWriteDataChunking) {\n  MockFtdi mock;\n  SpiMaster spi(&mock);\n\n  // 1MB is 65536 beats. Let's write 1MB + 16 bytes (65537 beats)\n  size_t len = (65535 + 2) * 16;\n  std::vector<uint8_t> payload(len, 0xDD);\n\n  // Simulation setup for multiple transactions\n  // Each write transaction waits for a dummy byte in the drain loop.\n  mock.to_read.push_back(0xFF);\n  mock.to_read.push_back(0xFF);\n\n  spi.v2_write_data(0x1000, payload.data(), len);\n\n  // We expect two v2_write_lines calls (one for 65535 beats, one for 2 beats)\n  // Each call has a header (7 bytes) + data + footer.\n  // Count how many times we see the v2 write header (0x02 as 7th byte of an\n  // MPSSE write stages 2 and 3 are\n  // passthroughs in this simplified, single-cycle implementation.\n  def FmaStage1(cmd: FmaCmd): FmaState1 = {\n    val out = Wire(new FmaState1)\n    out.out := compute(cmd.ina, cmd.inb, cmd.inc)\n    out\n  }\n\n  def FmaStage2(s1: FmaState1): FmaState2 = {\n    val out = Wire(new FmaState2)\n    out.out := s1.out\n    out\n  }\n\n  def FmaStage3(s2: FmaState2): Fp32 = {\n    s2.out\n  }\n\n  private def compute(a: Fp32, b: Fp32, c: Fp32): Fp32 = {\n    val out = Wire(new Fp32)\n\n    val anyNan = a.isNan() || b.isNan() || c.isNan()\n\n    val aZero = a.exponent === 0.U\n    val bZero = b.exponent === 0.U\n    val mulIsZero = aZero || bZero\n\n    val mulSign = a.sign ^ b.sign\n    val mantA = Cat(1.U(1.W), a.mantissa)  // 24 bits\n    val mantB = Cat(1.U(1.W), b.mantissa)  // 24 bits\n    val product = mantA * mantB             // 48 bits\n\n    // Sum of unbiased exponents = (a.exp - 127) + (b.exp - 127) = a.exp + b.exp - 254.\n    // Biased exp of product (before normalization) = a.exp + b.exp - 127.\n    val expSum = (a.exponent.zext +& b.exponent.zext) -& 127.S\n    // expSum is signed; size enough to hold range.\n\n    val highBit = product(47)\n    // After normalization the implicit-1 sits at bit 47. If highBit is 0, shift\n    // mantissa up by one (i.e. leave product as-is and decrement exponent).\n    val expProd = Mux(highBit, expSum + 1.S, expSum)\n    val mantProd49 = Mux(highBit, Cat(product, 0.U(1.W)), Cat(product, 0.U(1.W)) << 1)\n    // mantProd49 is now 49 bits with implicit 1 at bit 48 (or 0 if product was 0).\n    // Use the top 25 bits as G,R + mantissa:  bit 48 = implicit 1, bits 47..25 = mantissa, bit 24 = guard, bits 23..0 = sticky.\n\n    // Treat mulValue as a 49-bit signed magnitude (sign = mulSign), exponent = expProd.\n    // Now align with c.\n\n    // Build c's mantissa 49-bit-aligned the same way. Then add or subtract.\n    val cZero = c.exponent === 0.U && c.mantissa === 0.U\n    val mantC24 = Mux(c.exponent === 0.U, 0.U(24.W), Cat(1.U(1.W), c.mantissa))\n    // Place c at bit 48 of a 49-bit value, then below for rounding bits.\n    // c value as 49-bit: bits 48..25 = mantC24, bits 24..0 = 0.\n    val mantC49 = Cat(mantC24, 0.U(25.W))\n    val expC = c.exponent.zext\n\n    // Find larger exponent.\n    val expDiff = expProd - expC      // signed\n    val pBigger = expDiff >= 0.S\n\n    // Shift smaller right by |expDiff|, capped at 49 bits (anything beyond is sticky).\n    val absDiff = Mux(pBigger, expDiff, -expDiff).asUInt\n    val cap = 49.U\n    val shamt = Mux(absDiff > cap, cap, absDiff(5, 0))\n\n    // Shifted small value (with sticky-bit accumulation in LSB).\n    def shiftRightSticky(v: UInt, n: UInt): UInt = {\n      // Returns the right-shifted value with the LSB set if any bit fell off.\n      val mask = (1.U << n) - 1.U\n      val lostAny = (v & mask).orR\n      val shifted = v >> n\n      shifted | lostAny.asUInt\n    }\n\n    val pShifted = shiftRightSticky(mantProd49, Mux(pBigger, 0.U, shamt))\n    val cShifted = shiftRightSticky(mantC49,    Mux(pBigger, shamt, 0.U))\n\n    val finalExp = Mux(pBigger, expProd, expC)\n    val sameSign = mulSign === c.sign\n\n    // Add/sub mantissas. Operate in 50 bits to capture carry.\n    val sum50 = Wire(UInt(50.W))\n    val finalSign = Wire(Bool())\n\n    when (sameSign) {\n      sum50 := pShifted +& cShifted\n      finalSign := mulSign\n    } .otherwise {\n      val pGE = pShifted >= cShifted\n      sum50 := Mux(pGE, pShifted - cShifted, cShifted - pShifted).pad(50)\n      finalSign := Mux(pGE, mulSign, c.sign)\n    }\n\n    // Now normalize sum50. The implicit 1 should land at bit 48 (or, after\n    // an add carry, at bit 49).\n    val carry = sum50(49)\n    val normExp = Wire(SInt(12.W))\n    val normMant49 = Wire(UInt(49.W))\n\n    when (carry) {\n      // shift right by 1, increment exponent.\n      normMant49 := sum50(49, 1) | sum50(0).asUInt  // sticky preserves dropped LSB\n      normExp := finalExp + 1.S\n    } .otherwise {\n      // shift left until bit 48 is set; if the value is zero, leave it.\n      val v = sum50(48, 0)\n      // Find the leading-one position.\n      val isZero = v === 0.U\n      // Use PriorityEncoder over reverse to get leading zero count.\n      val rev = Reverse(v)\n      val lz = PriorityEncoder(rev)\n      val shiftAmt = Mux(isZero, 0.U, lz)\n      normMant49 := v << shiftAmt\n      normExp := finalExp - shiftAmt.zext\n    }\n\n    // Round to nearest, ties to even.\n    val implicit1 = normMant49(48)\n    val mant23 = normMant49(47, 25)\n    val guard = normMant49(24)\n    val round = normMant49(23)\n    val sticky = normMant49(22, 0).orR\n\n    val roundUp = guard && (round || sticky || mant23(0))\n    val mantSum = mant23 +& roundUp.asUInt\n    val mantOverflow = mantSum(23)\n    val mantFinal = mantSum(22, 0)\n    val expAfterRound = Mux(mantOverflow, normExp + 1.S, normExp)\n\n    // Decide overflow / underflow / zero.\n    val isZeroResult = !implicit1 && (mant23 === 0.U) && !roundUp\n    val overflow = expAfterRound >= 255.S\n    val underflow = expAfterRound <= 0.S\n\n    // When mul is zero, the result is c.\n    val cAsResult = Wire(new Fp32)\n    cAsResult := c\n\n    when (anyNan) {\n      out.sign := false.B\n      out.exponent := 0xFF.U\n      out.mantissa := (1.U(23.W) << 22)  // canonical NaN-ish\n    } .elsewhen (mulIsZero) {\n      // 0 * b + c = c (ignoring sign of zero, NaN). Even if c is also 0 we\n      // just propagate it.\n      out := cAsResult\n    } .elsewhen (overflow) {\n      out.sign := finalSign\n      out.exponent := 0xFF.U\n      out.mantissa := 0.U\n    } .elsewhen (underflow || isZeroResult) {\n      out.sign := finalSign\n      out.exponent := 0.U\n      out.mantissa := 0.U\n    } .otherwise {\n      out.sign := finalSign\n      out.exponent := expAfterRound(7, 0).asUInt\n      out.mantissa := mantFinal\n    }\n\n    out\n  }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1507,"cache_read_input_tokens":221803,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1507},"output_tokens":65,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c7ddc2fe-6b01-40a3-8e46-daa08a1b99f9","uuid":"856e2b91-123d-4ec9-98c0-23e344de222a"}

size enough to hold range.\n\n    val highBit = product(47)\n    // After normalization the implicit-1 sits at bit 47. If highBit is 0, shift\n    // mantissa up by one (i.e. leave product as-is and decrement exponent).\n    val expProd = Mux(highBit, expSum + 1.S, expSum)\n    val mantProd49 = Mux(highBit, Cat(product, 0.U(1.W)), Cat(product, 0.U(1.W)) << 1)\n    // mantProd49 is now 49 bits with implicit 1 at bit 48 (or 0 if product was 0).\n    // Use the top 25 bits as G,R + mantissa:  bit 48 = implicit 1, bits 47..25 = mantissa, bit 24 = guard, bits 23..0 = sticky.\n\n    // Treat mulValue as a 49-bit signed magnitude (sign = mulSign), exponent = expProd.\n    // Now align with c.\n\n    // Build c's mantissa 49-bit-aligned the same way. Operate in 50 bits to capture carry.\n    val sum50 = Wire(UInt(50.W))\n    val finalSign = Wire(Bool())\n\n    when (sameSign) {\n      sum50 := pShifted +& cShifted\n      finalSign := mulSign\n    } .otherwise {\n      val pGE = pShifted >= cShifted\n      sum50 := Mux(pGE, pShifted - cShifted, cShifted - pShifted).pad(50)\n      finalSign := Mux(pGE, mulSign, c.sign)\n    }\n\n    // Now normalize sum50. The implicit 1 should land at bit 48 (or, after\n    // an add carry, at bit 49).\n    val carry = sum50(49)\n    val normExp = Wire(SInt(12.W))\n    val normMant49 = Wire(UInt(49.W))\n\n    when (carry) {\n      // shift right by 1, increment exponent.\n      normMant49 := sum50(49, 1) | sum50(0).asUInt  // sticky preserves dropped LSB\n      normExp := finalExp + 1.S\n    } .otherwise {\n      // shift left until bit 48 is set; if the value is zero, leave it.\n      val v = sum50(48, 0)\n      // Find the leading-one position.\n      val isZero = v === 0.U\n      // Use PriorityEncoder over reverse to get leading zero count.\n      val rev = Reverse(v)\n      val lz = PriorityEncoder(rev)\n      val shiftAmt = Mux(isZero, 0.U, lz)\n      normMant49 := v << shiftAmt\n      normExp := finalExp - shiftAmt.zext\n    }\n\n    // Round to nearest, ties to even.\n    val implicit1 = normMant49(48)\n    val mant23 = normMant49(47, 25)\n    val guard = normMant49(24)\n    val round = normMant49(23)\n    val sticky = normMant49(22, 0).orR\n\n    val roundUp = guard && (round || sticky || mant23(0))\n    val mantSum = mant23 +& roundUp.asUInt\n    val mantOverflow = mantSum(23)\n    val mantFinal = mantSum(22, 0)\n    val expAfterRound = Mux(mantOverflow, normExp + 1.S, normExp)\n\n    // Decide overflow / underflow / zero.\n    val isZeroResult = !implicit1 && (mant23 === 0.U) && !roundUp\n    val overflow = expAfterRound >= 255.S\n    val underflow = expAfterRound <= 0.S\n\n    // When mul is zero, the result is c.\n    val cAsResult = Wire(new Fp32)\n    cAsResult := c\n\n    when (anyNan) {\n      out.sign := false.B\n      out.exponent := 0xFF.U\n      out.mantissa := (1.U(23.W) << 22)  // canonical NaN-ish\n    } .elsewhen (mulIsZero) {\n      // 0 * b + c = c (ignoring sign of zero, NaN). Even if c is also 0 we\n      // just propagate it.\n      out := cAsResult\n    } .elsewhen (overflow) {\n      out.sign := finalSign\n      out.exponent := 0xFF.U\n      out.mantissa := 0.U\n    } .elsewhen (underflow || isZeroResult) {\n      out.sign := finalSign\n      out.exponent := 0.U\n      out.mantissa := 0.U\n    } .otherwise {\n      out.sign := finalSign\n      out.exponent := expAfterRound(7, 0).asUInt\n      out.mantissa := mantFinal\n    }\n\n    out\n  }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":75,"oldLines":7,"newStart":75,"newLines":6,"lines":["     // Now align with c."," ","     // Build c's mantissa 49-bit-aligned the same way.
